TL;DR: 本研究利用共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）在La2-xSrxCuO4中发现了一种新的电子-声子耦合机制。在未掺杂样品中观察到约85、180和330 meV的尖锐激发峰，分别对应一阶、二阶和四阶Cu-O键拉伸声子模式，但仅在偶数阶声子出现显著强度，且与拉曼光谱形成鲜明对比。这种选择性耦合到偶数阶声子的现象无法用Franck-Condon框架解释，可能暗示绝缘铜氧化物中存在局域配对准粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强电子-声子耦合体系中声子高阶谐波的产生机制。传统理论框架（Franck-Condon包络）可解释晶体和分子中振动激发的高阶谐波强度分布，但在铜氧化物超导体中发现的异常电子-声子耦合行为无法用现有理论解释，需要新的实验证据去揭示这一新机制。

Method: 采用共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）技术对La2-xSrxCuO4进行系统性测量，覆盖未掺杂到10%空穴掺杂范围。通过分析不同能量位置的激发峰（约85、180和330 meV），结合声子色散关系和拉曼光谱对比，系统研究其掺杂依赖性以及共振激发的价电子与晶格振动的耦合特征。

Result: 在未掺杂样品中观察到三个尖锐激发峰（85±5, 180±7, 330±5 meV），无明显色散。其中180 meV和330 meV分别与面内Cu-O键拉伸模式的双声子和四声子激发能量近似一致，暗示共振激发电子与高阶声子过程强耦合。这些激发在10%空穴掺杂时消失，此时低能电荷激发由色散等离子体激元主导。观察到的选择性偶数阶声子耦合与拉曼光谱形成显著对比。

Conclusion: 该发现揭示了超越Franck-Condon图像的异常电子-声子耦合机制。选择性耦合到偶数阶声子的诡异行为提出了一种耐人寻味的可能性——绝缘铜氧化物中可能存在局域配对准粒子，这为理解高温超导机制提供了新的视角和实验线索。

Abstract: In crystals and molecules with strong coupling between the charge carriers and atomic vibrations, high order harmonics of the vibrational excitations can be observed with intensity following the Franck-Condon envelope. Here, we uncover a new regime of electron-phonon coupling in La2-xSrxCuO4 using resonant inelastic X-ray scattering. In the undoped compound, we find sharp peaks at approximately 85$\pm$5 meV, 180$\pm$7 meV, and 330$\pm$5 meV with no observable dispersion, and an absence of appreciable intensity from other modes observed in Raman spectroscopy in this energy range. The 180 meV and 330 meV excitations have energies approximately consistent with two-phonon and four-phonon in-plane Cu-O bond stretching mode, suggesting that the resonantly excited valence electrons strongly couple to this mode. These excitations exhibit significant doping dependence, eventually becoming unresolvable at 10% hole doping, where the low energy charge excitations are dominated by dispersive plasmons. The observation of selective coupling to even-ordered phonons and stark contrast with the Raman spectra indicate anomalous electron-phonon coupling beyond the Franck-Condon picture. One intriguing possibility is the existence of locally paired quasiparticles in insulating cuprates.

</details>


### [98] [Textures as a phase-transition probe for quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08120)
*Heitor P. Casagrande,Isaac M. Carvalho,William J. Munro,Krissia Zawadzki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子纹理中的"褶皱度"(rugosity)度量在量子相变探测中的应用，展示了其作为简单有效的相变探测工具的价值。作者建立了计算基下褶皱度与自旋关联函数层级之间的联系，并分析了全局基态与不同磁化扇区基态（全局vs对称分辨褶皱度）下的褶皱度，用于研究海森堡XXZ模型的相图。研究发现两个转变点均出现不同的褶皱度特征，尤其是在Δ=1处即使对于小系统也出现了尖锐的特征，表明纹理编码的关联层级对该点的高度敏感性。由于BKT转变与XXZ模型的各向同性SU(2)点重合，该行为可能反映了褶皱度对各向同性点处自旋关联层级结构的特殊敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纹理作为一种新兴工具已被用于量化相干性和量子门识别，本研究旨在探索其扩展到量子相变探测领域的可行性，特别是通过褶皱度度量来有效探测相变，并理解其在XXZ模型相图中的行为。

Method: 作者建立了计算基下褶皱度与自旋关联函数层级（spin correlators hierarchy）之间的理论联系，并系统比较了全局基态褶皱度与不同磁化扇区中的对称分辨褶皱度，通过对海森堡XXZ模型相图的分析验证该方法的有效性。

Result: 在XXZ模型的两个相变点处均观察到不同的褶皱度特征。特别地，在Δ=1处即使对于小系统也出现了尖锐的褶皱度特征，显示出关联层级纹理对该各向同性点的显著敏感性；这种敏感性可能与BKT转变与SU(2)各向同性点的重合有关。

Conclusion: 褶皱度度量可作为量子相变探测的有效工具，且其对自旋关联层级结构的敏感性使其能够捕获XXZ模型中BKT转变等精细的物理特征，为量子相变研究提供了一种简洁的新视角。

Abstract: The idea of quantum texture has been recently proposed and used as a tool for quantifying coherences and for quantum gate identification. In this work we offer a study on its usage to quantum phase transitions, demonstrating the rugosity metric as a simple tool for effective phase-transition probing. We establish the link between rugosity in the computational basis and the hierarchy of spin correlators, and analyze rugosities defined in the global ground-state and in ground-states belonging to different magnetization sectors (to which we refer to as global vs symmetry-resolved rugosities) to study the phase diagram of the Heisenberg XXZ model. We find distinct rugosity signatures at both transition points. In particular, a sharp feature appears at $Δ=1$ already for small systems, revealing a pronounced sensitivity of the correlation hierarchy encoded by the texture to this point. Since the BKT transition coincides with the isotropic $SU(2)$ point of the XXZ model, this behavior may reflect a particular sensitivity of rugosity to the structure of the spin-correlation hierarchy at isotropy.

</details>


### [99] [Observation of multipartite spin entanglement in a cuprate chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08134)
*S. F. R. TenHuisen,Z. Shen,V. Bhartiya,V. Menon,P. Sharma,H. Padma,Z. Guan,W. He,M. K. Lajer,J. Li,D. Banerjee,J. Pelliciari,I. A. Zaliznyak,G. D. Gu,M. D. Lukin,S. Johnston,M. P. M. Dean,V. Bisogni,Y. Wang,M. Mitrano*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）实现了对量子材料中多体自旋纠缠的直接探测，在准一维铜氧化物Sr₂CuO₃中检测到至少7方自旋纠缠，这是固体材料中报道的最大纠缠深度之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料被认为蕴含高度纠缠的量子态，但如何实验探测并量化这种纠缠长期以来一直难以实现。

Method: 使用RIXS作为多体自旋纠缠探针，通过将RIXS截面投影到相应的自旋激发通道，直接从测量的自旋涨落谱中提取量子Fisher信息。

Result: 在模型一维铜酸盐Sr₂CuO₃中检测到至少7粒子多体自旋纠缠，这是固体中已报道的最大纠缠深度之一；观测到的纠缠深度与Hubbard模型预期一致，且在高温下仍然保持。

Conclusion: 该项工作确立了RIXS作为多体自旋纠缠的实验探测手段，并为将纠缠探测扩展到电荷、轨道以及非平衡态体系提供了平台。

Abstract: Quantum materials are believed to host highly entangled states of matter, but probing and quantifying such entanglement has long remained experimentally elusive. Here, we use resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) as a probe of multipartite spin entanglement. By projecting the RIXS cross section onto the appropriate spin excitation channel, we show that the Quantum Fisher Information can be extracted directly from the measured spin fluctuation spectrum. We detect at least 7-partite spin entanglement in the model one-dimensional cuprate Sr$_2$CuO$_3$, revealing one of the largest entanglement depths yet reported in a solid. The observed entanglement depth agrees with Hubbard-model expectations and persists to elevated temperatures. Our results establish RIXS as an experimental probe of many-body spin entanglement and provide a platform for extensions to charge and orbital sectors and nonequilibrium settings.

</details>


### [100] [Successive Phase Transitions from a Composite Fermion Liquid to a Fractional Quantum Hall State at ν=3/2 Driven by In-Plane Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08141)
*Xinghao Wang,L. N. Pfeiffer,A. Gupta,K. W. Baldwin,K. W. West,Rui-Rui Du*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索偶数分母分数量子霍尔态的产生机制，特别是如何在不需要传统朗道能级填充的条件下，通过面内磁场诱导的费米面劈裂来实现拓扑序的工程化调控。

Method: 在超高迁移率GaAs量子阱中进行系统性输运测量，通过逐步增大面内磁场B_||，观察系统在ν=3/2填充因子下的相变行为，包括从复合费米子液体到软能隙FQH态再到硬能隙FQH态的两次转变，同时探测子态的出现。

Result: 在B_||约12.2 T时观察到从复合费米子液体到软能隙FQH态的转变，在B_||约14.7 T时发生拓扑相变到硬能隙稳健FQH态，并在该过程中发现了ν=19/13子态。提供了系统的实验数据支持这些发现。

Conclusion: 该工作证明可以通过面内磁场实现k空间费米面轮廓劈裂来工程化拓扑序，为理解和调控偶数分母分数量子霍尔态提供了新的途径和物理解释框架。

Abstract: We report an even-denominator fractional quantum Hall state at ν = 3/2 induced entirely by in-plane magnetic field B_|| in an ultra-high-mobility GaAs quantum well. As B_|| increases, the system undergoes two successive transitions: from a composite fermion liquid to a soft-gap FQH state (B_||~12.2 T), then via a topological phase transition to a hard-gap robust FQH state (B_||~14.7 T), accompanied by a daughter state at ν = 19/13. We present systematic data, and discuss a possible scenario in interpreting these findings. Our work demonstrates that topological order may be engineered through k-space Fermi contour splitting under an in-plane magnetic field.

</details>


### [101] [Kane-Lubensky phonons in Maxwell lattice frustrated Mott insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08262)
*Hong-Hao Song,Gang v. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示在三维Maxwell晶格阻挫Mott绝缘体中，通过磁拓扑力学机制可以产生Kane-Lubensky型零能Weyl线声子。在烧绿石反铁磁体中，磁场选择的自旋态引起晶格畸变形成P4_3 32拓扑晶格，自旋-晶格耦合与自旋构型使邻近键弯曲，将系统转变为拓扑Maxwell晶格，从而承载体零频Weyl线声子（非Goldstone模），其特征低温比热为C_ph~T^2（区别于传统Goldstein声子的T^3）。该发现可应用于含Cr尖晶石体系，并为大量拓扑零频声子与其它自由度强耦合提供低能平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑力学系统主要关注有限频率的拓扑声子，而零频率的非Goldstone拓扑声子鲜有研究。作者旨在探索能否在三维真实磁性材料体系中实现Kane-Lubensky型的零能Weyl线声子，并揭示其独特的物理性质（如非常规比热标度）及潜在应用前景。

Method: 以烧绿石反铁磁体的自旋-晶格耦合模型为基础：1) 磁场选择特定自旋态，诱导晶格畸变形成P4_3 32拓扑晶体结构；2) 自旋-晶格耦合与自旋排列导致邻近化学键弯曲，使系统满足Maxwell晶格的等距约束条件实现拓扑分类；3) 结合拓扑力学理论（Kane-Lubensky框架）分析零模的体态拓扑保护性质，并通过热力学计算（声子比热）验证其低能物理特征。

Result: 系统成功承载拓扑保护的体零频Weyl线声子，这些零频模式不属于Goldstone模。一维零模流形导致独特的低温声子比热C_ph~T^2（而非常规Goldstone声子的T^3），展现了零频拓扑声子的非常规热力学响应。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了真实磁性材料（如Cr基尖晶石体系）中实现零频拓扑力学的可行途径，为大量拓扑零频声子与其他自由度（电子、自旋等）的强耦合提供了理论平台，有望催生奇特声子介导的新物理现象及应用。

Abstract: We show that zero-energy gapless Weyl-line phonons of the Kane-Lubensky's type can arise in the three-dimensional Maxwell lattice frustrated Mott insulators through the magnetopological mechanics. In a pyrochlore antiferromagnet with the spin-lattice coupling, a magnetic field selects the spin state whose lattice distortion generates a $P4_3 32$ topological lattice. More crucially, the spin-lattice coupling and the spin configuration cause the bending of the neighbouring bonds, and converts the system into the topological Maxwell lattice. Remarkably, the resulting system is found to host the bulk zero-frequency Weyl-line phonons protected topologically, and these gapless phonons are not Goldstone modes. Unlike the conventional ${C_{\rm ph}\sim T^3}$ for the Goldstone phonons, these one-dimensional zero-mode manifolds yield a characteristic low-temperature phonon specific heat ${C_{\rm ph}\sim T^2}$. Our results could find applications in the Cr-based spinel systems, and moreover, we establish a low-energy platform where an extensive number of topological zero-frequency phonons can strongly couple to other degrees of freedom, opening a route to exotic phonon-mediated phenomena.

</details>


### [102] [Theory of Kondo hybridization wave in Kondo lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08467)
*Yin Zhong*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于大-N费米子平均场理论，在正方晶格Kondo晶格模型中系统研究了Kondo杂化波(KHW)序的形成机制，建立了基态相图，发现Q=(0,π)的KHW相可以复现UTe₂和1T/1H-TaS₂中观测到的空间调制特征，并预言了区分不同KHW态的电导率各向异性输运指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描隧道显微镜实验在重费米子超导体UTe₂和人工Kondo晶格体系1T/1H-TaS₂中发现了Emergent空间调制的Kondo杂化波(KHW)序，这挑战了重费米子物理中Kondo杂化空间均匀的传统范式，但缺乏相应的微观理论解释。

Method: 基于正方晶格Kondo晶格模型，采用大-N费米子平均场近似，系统识别稳定的调制KHW相并建立基态相图，通过对比KHW相的Kondo杂化能隙空间调制模式与实验观测，并计算面内电导率各向异性来预测输运行为。

Result: 发现Q=(0,π)的KHW相具有单轴条带状的Kondo杂化能隙调制，可忠实复现UTe₂中的空间调制图案；其固有的晶胞加倍调制精确解释了1T/1H-TaS₂中的谱学特征；并预言了可用于区分不同KHW波矢态的特征性面内电导率各向异性。

Conclusion: 该工作为KHW序提供了微观理论基础，建立了理解重费米子材料中emergent调制杂化现象的统一理论框架，并给出了可验证的实验预言。

Abstract: Recent scanning tunneling microscopy experiments have discovered emergent spatially modulated Kondo hybridization wave (KHW) order in the heavy fermion superconductor UTe$_2$ and the artificial Kondo lattice system 1T/1H-TaS$_2$, challenging the conventional paradigm of spatially uniform Kondo hybridization in heavy fermion physics. Here, we develop a microscopic theory for KHW order based on the canonical square-lattice Kondo lattice model within the large-$N$ fermionic mean-field approximation. We systematically identify stable modulated KHW phases and establish their ground-state phase diagram. The prominent $\boldsymbol{Q}=(0,π)$ KHW phase yields uniaxial stripe modulation of the Kondo hybridization gap, which faithfully reproduces the spatial modulation pattern observed in UTe$_2$. Moreover, its inherent unit-cell-doubling modulation precisely accounts for the spectroscopic features measured in 1T/1H-TaS$_2$. We further predict a characteristic in-plane conductivity anisotropy that serves as a definitive transport fingerprint to discriminate KHW states with distinct ordering wavevectors. Our work provides a microscopic foundation for the newly observed KHW order and establishes a unified theoretical framework for understanding emergent modulated hybridization phenomena in heavy fermion materials.

</details>


### [103] [High-resolution angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy with tunable magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08498)
*Jairo Obando-Guevara,Huu-Thong Le,Emiliano Corcino Aracena,Sayan Singha,Kyungchan Lee,Yingdong Guan,Zhiqiang Mao,Dezhe Jin,Chaoxing Liu,Heike Pfau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示了在强电子轨迹像差存在的情况下，通过后处理电子轨迹模拟，可从磁光电子能谱（magneto-ARPES）中高精度重构电子结构，并保持高动量分辨率。以Bi₂Se₃在偶极场中的实验为例，确定了二维动量映射、微米级光束斑点和精确数值场模拟是关键技术要求，为更大磁场下的magneto-ARPES提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场对量子现象的调控是材料研究中不可或缺的工具。虽然magneto-ARPES实现了原位磁场调控下的动量与能量分辨测量，但电子轨迹像差在即使很小的磁场下也会导致严重的光谱畸变和展宽，限制了该技术的应用。因此需要解决磁场引起的轨迹像差问题，实现高分辨率磁光电子能谱测量。

Method: 使用激光基ARPES系统研究偶极场中的Bi₂Se₃样品，通过详细的电子轨迹模拟在后处理中重构电子结构；利用圆二色性补充磁场与自旋耦合的信息；通过模拟的标度律分析关键实验参数。

Result: 即使在强轨迹像差存在下，电子结构仍能以高精度和高动量分辨率被重构。识别出二维动量映射、微米级光束斑点尺寸和精确数值场模拟是达到高分辨率magneto-ARPES的三个关键技术要求，并展示了圆二色性可提供磁场-自旋耦合的额外信息。

Conclusion: 实验结果与模拟标度律为在更大磁场下开展magneto-ARPES提供了路线图，表明该技术有望进一步拓展至更强场条件下的电子结构研究。

Abstract: The control and perturbation of quantum phenomena with magnetic fields is an indispensable tool in materials research. Recently, in-situ field tuning was implemented into angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (magneto-ARPES), which provides direct momentum and energy-resolved information of the field-dependent electronic structure. However, aberrations of the electron trajectories were shown to be substantial, leading to significant spectral distortions and broadening even in small fields. Here we show that the electronic structure can be recovered from magneto-ARPES spectra with high accuracy while maintaining high momentum resolution even when strong trajectory aberrations are present. We studied Bi$_2$Se$_3$ in a dipole field of a coil using a laser-based ARPES system. The electronic structure was reconstructed in post-processing using detailed electron trajectory simulations. We identify two-dimensional (2D) momentum mapping, a micron beam spot size, and precise numerical field simulations as critical technical requirements for high-resolution magneto-ARPES. We show how circular dichroism can provide additional information about the coupling of the magnetic field to the spin. The experimental achievements and the scaling laws from our simulations provide a road map towards magneto-ARPES in larger fields.

</details>


### [104] [Schwinger boson perturbation theory for spin-$S$ Kitaev-Heisenberg magnets: phase diagram and dynamical response near Kitaev spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08548)
*Daiki Sasamoto,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了自旋-S Kitaev-Heisenberg模型的Schwinger玻色子微扰框架，以纯Kitaev模型的自旋液体鞍点作为未微扰态，利用Klein对偶性将哈密顿量精确分解，通过随机相位近似确定不同自旋值(S=1/2, 1, 3/2, 2)下量子自旋液体与磁有序相之间的相边界，并计算了自旋动力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev-Heisenberg模型在不同自旋尺度下的量子自旋液体稳定性及其向磁有序相的转变机制尚不清楚，特别是自旋S>1/2时相边界和自旋动力学的定量描述仍缺乏有效框架。

Method: 以纯Kitaev模型的自旋液体鞍点作为未微扰态构型Schwinger玻色子微扰框架；利用Kitaev-Heisenberg模型固有的Klein对偶性将哈密顿量精确分为两部分：在对偶变换下不变的部分作为未微扰项，改变符号的部分作为微扰项，并进行随机相位近似(RPA)计算。

Result: 确定了S=1/2, 1, 3/2和2时量子自旋液体与相邻磁有序相的相边界；自旋液体区域随S增大迅速收缩，在S=2时变得非常狭窄。对于S=1的自旋动力学计算表明，重整化后的动力学自旋结构因子在有限能量处保留宽的双自旋子连续谱，而低能谱权重在相邻磁相的序波矢处软化。

Conclusion: 该框架使得在热力学极限下计算磁失稳附近的自旋动力学成为可能，为理解Kitaev-Heisenberg模型中自旋尺度对量子自旋液体稳定性和激发谱的影响提供了系统性的理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a Schwinger boson perturbative framework for the spin-$S$ Kitaev-Heisenberg model. The spin-liquid saddle point of the pure Kitaev model is used as the unperturbed state, and magnetic instabilities and dynamical spin correlations are evaluated around this saddle point. We decompose the Hamiltonian exactly into two parts by exploiting the Klein duality intrinsic to the Kitaev-Heisenberg model. We perform the random-phase approximation by taking the part invariant under the duality transformation as the unperturbed term and treating the remaining term, which changes sign under it, as the perturbation. We determine the phase boundaries separating the quantum spin-liquid regimes from the adjacent magnetically ordered phases for $S=1/2$, $1$, $3/2$, and $2$. The spin-liquid regions shrink rapidly with increasing $S$ and become very narrow at $S=2$. We also compute the spin dynamics of the spin-$S$ Kitaev-Heisenberg model, focusing on $S=1$, and find that the dressed dynamical spin structure factor retains a broad two-spinon continuum at finite energies, while the low-energy spectral weight softens at the ordering wave vectors of the adjacent magnetic phases. Our framework thus enables thermodynamic-limit calculations of spin dynamics near magnetic instabilities.

</details>


### [105] [Magnetic phases of Kondo lattice materials Ce$_5$RhGe$_2$ and Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08580)
*Jiawen Zhang,Yanan Zhang,Mingyi Wang,Ye Chen,Yu Liu,Yongjun Zhang,Michael Smidman,Huiqiu Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Ce5RhGe2和Ce5IrGe2单晶的电输运、比热和磁化强度，并与Ce5CoGe2对比。三者均具有正交Pnma结构，晶格参数随Co→Rh→Ir单调增大（负化学压力效应）。Ce5RhGe2在约11.5 K呈现铁磁有序，磁行为与Ce5CoGe2相似；而Ce5IrGe2在12.7 K和11.8 K发生两次连续磁相变，沿易磁化轴方向呈现多个变磁转变及Ms/5和Ms/3磁化平台，最终在最低温度下表现为亚铁磁基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过系统对比Ce5TGe2（T=Co, Rh, Ir）系列化合物，研究负化学压力（晶格膨胀）对铈基化合物磁性能的影响，探索该体系中从铁磁到亚铁磁的磁性基态演化规律。

Method: 制备Ce5RhGe2和Ce5IrGe2单晶，采用电阻率、比热和磁化强度测量，沿三个主晶轴方向进行磁化测量，并结合变温/变场下的变磁转变和磁滞回线分析来确定磁性基态。

Result: 所有化合物中a轴为易磁化方向。Ce5RhGe2为铁磁体（TC≈11.5 K），行为类似Ce5CoGe2；Ce5IrGe2具有两个磁转变（TM1=12.7 K、TM2=11.8 K），沿易磁化轴观察到多重变磁转变，形成Ms/5和Ms/3磁化平台，低场变磁转变随温度降低而向更低场移动，并最终在零场附近出现明显磁滞回线，表明其低温基态为亚铁磁性。

Conclusion: Ce5TGe2系列中从Co到Ir的化学替换通过负化学压力效应调节磁交换相互作用，导致磁性从铁磁（Co, Rh）演变为亚铁磁（Ir），揭示了晶格参数变化对该体系磁基态的关键调控作用。

Abstract: Single crystals of Ce$_5$RhGe$_2$ and Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$ have been systematically investigated by electrical resistivity, specific heat, and magnetization measurements. Together with Ce$_5$CoGe$_2$, all three compounds crystallize in the orthorhombic \emph{Pnma} structure, with the lattice parameters increasing monotonically from Co to Rh to Ir, consistent with the effect of negative chemical pressure. Magnetization measurements along the three principal crystallographic axes identify the \emph{a} axis as the easy magnetization direction throughout the series. Ce$_5$RhGe$_2$ exhibits ferromagnetic ordering with a Curie temperature of approximately 11.5 K and shows magnetic behavior closely resembling that of Ce$_5$CoGe$_2$. In contrast, Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$ undergoes two successive magnetic transitions at $T_{\rm M1}=12.7$ K and $T_{\rm M2}=11.8$ K, and there are multiple metamagnetic transitions under magnetic fields, giving rise to magnetization plateaus at fractions of the saturation magnetization $M_{\rm s}$ of approximately $M_{\rm s}/5$ and $M_{\rm s}/3$. The low-field metamagnetic transition along the easy axis shifts to lower field with decreasing temperature, and eventually a pronounced hysteresis loop is observed about zero-field, establishing that Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$ exhibits a ferrimagnetic ground state at the lowest measured temperatures.

</details>


### [106] [$g$-wave altermagnetic order parameter in hematite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08693)
*Tianren Wang,Yuehong Li,Yu Feng,Andong Liu,Yuetong Wu,Qian Zhao,Yujie Yan,Wei Luo,Xin Tong,Yi Lu,Yao Shen,Stefano Agrestini,Jaewon Choi,Qisi Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用非弹性中子散射在赤铁矿（α-Fe₂O₃）中定量测定了交错磁性（altermagnetic）磁振子劈裂的动量空间对称性，发现劈裂沿节线方向消失并在离节处达到最大，揭示了g波对称的交替磁性序参量。实验结果与线性自旋波理论一致，表明非简并分支为相反手性的磁振子，劈裂源于对称不等价的长程交换相互作用。这是首次对交替磁性手性磁振子的动量空间对称性进行定量测定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体结合了反铁磁体的零净磁化与动量依赖的自旋劈裂，磁振子能带劈裂可提供交替磁序的直接探针，并可能实现手性选择性磁振子输运。然而，这种劈裂的动量空间对称性此前尚未被定量确定，这是理解交替磁性磁振子性质和应用潜力的关键缺失环节。

Method: 采用非弹性中子散射技术，在赤铁矿（α-Fe₂O₃）中系统地映射交替磁性磁振子劈裂随动量的变化关系，并将实验数据与基于交替磁性模型的线性自旋波理论计算结果进行对比验证。

Result: 实验观测到磁振子劈裂沿节线（节向）方向消失，并在离节点区域达到最大值，清晰揭示了g波对称的交替磁性序参量。线性自旋波理论计算与实验结果高度吻合，进一步将非简并的磁振子分支识别为相反手性的磁振子，并追溯劈裂的起源为对称不等价的长程交换相互作用。

Conclusion: 本研究首次定量确定了交替磁性手性磁振子的动量空间对称性。结合赤铁矿的高磁有序温度和低磁振子阻尼特性，该材料被确立为低耗散、对称选择性磁振子应用的理想平台。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine the vanishing net magnetization of antiferromagnets with momentum-dependent spin splitting. Magnon band splitting provides a direct probe of altermagnetic order and may enable chirality-selective magnon transport, yet the momentum-space symmetry of this splitting has not been determined quantitatively. Here we use inelastic neutron scattering to map the momentum dependence of altermagnetic magnon splitting in hematite ($α$-Fe$_2$O$_3$). The splitting vanishes along nodal directions and reaches maxima off the nodes, revealing the $g$-wave symmetry of the altermagnetic order parameter. These results agree with linear spin-wave theory calculations based on the altermagnetic model, which further identify the nondegenerate branches as magnons of opposite chirality and trace the splitting to symmetry-inequivalent long-range exchange interactions. Our results provide the first quantitative determination of the momentum-space symmetry of altermagnetic chiral magnons. These findings, together with hematite's high magnetic ordering temperature and low magnon damping, establish it as a promising platform for low-dissipation, symmetry-selective magnonic applications.

</details>


### [107] [Examining Convergence of Cluster Perturbation Theory and Cluster Coupled Cluster for J1-J2 Heisenberg Spin Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08768)
*Jeffrey Keyes,Carlos Jimenez-Hoyos*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文将簇平均场（cMF）波函数作为微扰论和耦合簇的零阶波函数，将此前对J1-J2海森堡模型的计算从cPT4和cCCSD扩展到cPT7和cCCSDTQ，验证了能量收敛性，并讨论了在热力学极限下高效执行簇基计算的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cMF的核心主张是将强关联系统转化为弱关联系统，从而使基于弱关联的微扰论和耦合簇方法得以使用。为验证这一主张，需要证明PT和CC序列确实收敛，而此前工作仅止步于cPT4和cCCSD，缺乏对序列收敛性的充分验证。

Method: 将cMF波函数作为零阶波函数，将微扰论（PT）扩展到第七阶（cPT7），将耦合簇扩展到完整的五重激发（cCCSDTQ5），并针对热力学极限下的簇基计算提出了高效实现方案以支撑更昂贵的计算。

Result: 在高阶微扰论（cPT7）和完整五重激发耦合簇（cCCSDTQ5）水平上，能量显示收敛趋势，从而验证了cMF方法的中心主张；同时解决了热力学极限下簇基计算的高效性问题。

Conclusion: cMF作为零阶波函数的PT和CC系列均表现出收敛性，证实了cMF确实能将强关联问题转化为适合弱关联方法处理的弱关联问题，并且簇基方法在热力学极限下的高效实现是可行的。

Abstract: In previous work, it was shown that using the cluster mean field(cMF) wavefunction as the zeroth order wavefunction for perturbation theory and coupled cluster produces a reasonable approximation for the J 1-J 2 Heisenberg model in the thermodynamic limit(TDL). However, this work was limited to cPT4 and cCCSD. The central claim of cMF is that it takes strongly correlated systems and makes them weakly correlated so that PT weakly correlated methods can be used. To confirm that this claim is true, it is important to show that the PT and CC series converge. In this paper, we extend the previous calculations to cPT7 and cCCSDTQ5 where we show that the energy is converging. We also discuss how to perform cluster based calculations efficiently in the thermodynamic limit, which is crucial for these more expensive calculations.

</details>


### [108] [Positive Temperature Coefficient of Anisotropy due to $d^6L$ Groundstate in $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08927)
*Rachel Nickel,Kurt Kummer,Johan van Lierop*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了ε-Fe₂O₃及其Cr掺杂体系（削弱Fe-O杂化）中正各向异性温度系数(dK/dT>0)的起源，发现该现象由金属-配体杂化驱动而非传统的强自旋轨道耦合子系统。纯相中识别出d⁶L基态，Cr掺杂后变为d⁶+d⁶L²，表明金属-配体杂化可作为调控磁性、电子和轨道特性的有效途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，正各向异性温度系数(dK/dT>0)通常源于磁性3d态与强自旋轨道耦合(SOC)子系统的杂化。然而，ε-Fe₂O₃在125-200K区间表现出dK/dT>0，却不含有明显的SOC合作伙伴，这挑战了现有理论框架。研究旨在揭示这种反常正各向异性温度系数的微观机制。

Method: 对比研究纯ε-Fe₂O₃和Cr掺杂ε-Fe₂O₃（通过Cr掺杂削弱Fe-O杂化），跨越低温非公度磁相到高各向异性磁相的相变过程进行系统分析，研究金属-配体杂化强度变化对各向异性和电子结构的影响。

Result: 纯ε-Fe₂O₃中识别出d⁶L基态（L为配体空穴），表明金属-配体电荷转移在基态中起关键作用；Cr掺杂削弱Fe-O杂化后，体系转变为d⁶+d⁶L²混合组态。这直接证明金属-配体杂化是该材料正各向异性温度系数的根源。

Conclusion: 金属-配体杂化（而非传统SOC伙伴子系统）是ε-Fe₂O₃中dK/dT>0现象的微观起源，为通过杂化工程（如掺杂调控）来设计磁性、电子和轨道特性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Positive anisotropy temperature coefficients ($dK/dT>0$) usually arise from hybridization between magnetic 3d states and strongly spin-orbit-coupled (SOC) subsystems. Yet $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ shows $dK/dT>0$ (125-200~K) without an obvious SOC partner. We study pure and Cr-doped (with weakened Fe-O hybridization) $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ across the transition from low temperature incommensurate to high-anisotropy magnetic phases. We identify a $d^6L$ groundstate in $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ while reduced hybridization yields $d^6 + d^6L^2$ in the Cr-doped system, highlighting metal-ligand hybridization as a route to tune magnetic, electronic and orbitronic properties.

</details>


### [109] [Real-time Dyson expansion for the nonequilibrium $GW$ approximation: Correlated spectra and dependence on the reference propagator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08932)
*Erik Schroedter,Jan-Philip Joost,Michael Bonitz,Vojtech Vlcek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文扩展了实时Dyson展开（RT-DE）方法，使其适用于非平衡GW自能，支持具有时间局域离对角演化的通用参考传播子（Hartree-Fock、静态屏蔽交换及基于HF-GKBA的相关传播子）。在驱动双带晶格模型中与精确对角化对比验证，发现平均场和静态屏蔽参考能最准确再现谱（包括卫星结构），而GKBA方法在散射诱导占据数动力学上更优但会人为展宽/劈裂谱峰。该方法可应用于超越精确方法的大系统，解析出价带的激子复制峰和激子shake-up卫星结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡GW近似的全双时模拟随时间三次方增长，计算代价高昂；而时间局域近似或广义Kadanoff-Baym假设通常只在谱中保留平均场特征。此前RT-DE虽能以时间线性代价恢复动力学关联，但仅局限于第二阶Born近似且参考传播子为平均场形式。因此需要将RT-DE推广到非平衡GW自能并探索更优的参考传播子选择。

Method: 在非平衡Green函数框架中，将RT-DE方法扩展到非平衡GW自能，求解的参考传播子具有通用形式但离对角演化为时间局域。具体考察三类参考：Hartree-Fock传播子、含非平衡屏蔽的静态屏蔽交换传播子、以及基于Hartree-Fock GKBA的相关传播子。用驱动双带长程相互作用晶格模型的精确对角化结果进行基准验证。

Result: 基准测试表明：Hartree-Fock与静态屏蔽参考能得到最准确的谱，包括平均场水平缺失的卫星结构；GKBA参考最佳捕捉散射诱导的占据数动力学，但会人为地展宽和劈裂谱峰。在大系统应用中，该方法解析出价带的激子复制峰以及激子shake-up卫星，并揭示了这些结构随激发密度增加的形变。

Conclusion: 该工作将RT-DE从第二阶Born近似推广到含GW自能的通用情形，系统比较了不同参考传播子的优劣，明确了各有侧重：谱精度的最佳参考是HF或静态屏蔽交换，而占据数动态的最佳捕捉依赖GKBA参考。该方法为超越精确对角化的大系统中研究非平衡谱和激子卫星结构提供了可行的线性标度计算工具。

Abstract: Electronic spectra provide direct insight into the excitations and correlations of condensed matter systems. Their description requires electron correlations beyond mean field. In equilibrium, the $GW$ approximation has become the method of choice for many materials. Extending this approximation to nonequilibrium, however, is challenging. Full two-time $GW$ simulations within the nonequilibrium Green's functions approach scale at least cubically with propagation time, whereas improved scaling schemes such as time-local (adiabatic) approximations or the generalized Kadanoff--Baym ansatz usually retain only mean-field character in the spectra. The recently introduced real-time Dyson expansion recovers dynamical correlations in the spectrum at time-linear cost, but has so far been restricted to the second-order Born approximation with mean-field reference propagators. Here we formulate and implement the RT-DE for the nonequilibrium $GW$ self-energy with reference propagators of general form whose off-diagonal evolution is time local. In particular, we assess Hartree--Fock propagators, propagators with statically screened exchange with nonequilibrium screening, and a correlated propagator based on the Hartree--Fock GKBA. Benchmarks against exact diagonalization for a driven two-band lattice model with long-range interactions show that the mean-field and statically screened references yield the most accurate spectra, including satellite structures absent at mean-field level, whereas the GKBA-based approach best captures scattering-induced occupation dynamics, but artificially broadens and splits spectral peaks. When applied to large systems that are beyond the reach of exact methods, the scheme resolves the excitonic replica of the valence band and satellites identified as exciton shake-up, as well as their reshaping with increasing excitation density.

</details>


### [110] [On spectral properties of Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09067)
*Miroslav Hopjan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义Kadanoff-Baym Ansatz（GKBA）在非平衡量子系统模拟中的局限性。通过扩展GKBA方法，作者在Hubbard团簇中恢复了GKBA碰撞积分中被隐藏的谱信息，并与标准Kadanoff-Baym方程进行对比。结果表明：中等相互作用强度下两者吻合良好，但在强相互作用区域出现显著偏差，扩展GKBA得到的谱函数甚至会变为负值，揭示了GKBA在强关联体系中的重要局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Kadanoff-Baym Ansatz因其计算效率优势（计算成本随传播时间线性增长）被广泛用于模拟非平衡费米子和玻色子系统，但其时间对角结构会导致输运方程碰撞积分中的谱性质被遮蔽/丢失。作者希望恢复并研究这些隐藏的谱性质，以评估GKBA在强相互作用体系下的适用性边界。

Method: 采用扩展广义Kadanoff-Baym Ansatz（extended GKBA）方法，在Hubbard团簇模型中恢复被标准GKBA隐藏的谱信息；将扩展GKBA计算的基态谱函数与完整Kadanoff-Baym方程（KBE）的结果进行系统对比，分别在中等和强相互作用强度下检验两类方法的谱函数一致性。

Result: （1）在中等相互作用强度下，扩展GKBA与标准KBE得到的谱函数吻合良好；（2）在强相互作用强度下，两者出现显著偏差，且扩展GKBA得到的谱函数出现负值——即非物理结果。这表明GKBA在强关联区域会产生非物理的谱性质。

Conclusion: 尽管GKBA具有显著的计算优势，但它在强相互作用区域存在重要局限：当相互作用足够强时，GKBA可能产生非物理的谱性质（如负谱函数）。这界定了GKBA类方法的有效适用范围，也提示在实际应用中需要对相互作用强度加以甄别或辅以更严格的方法交叉验证。

Abstract: The Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz with mean-field propagators is increasingly used to simulate non-equilibrium quantum fermionic and bosonic systems. Compared with the full Kadanoff--Baym equations, its mean-field propagators substantially reduce computational cost, enabling simulations with computational effort that scales linearly with propagation time. However, the time-diagonal structure of the Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz obscures spectral properties within the collision integral of the transport equation. Here, we recover and investigate these hidden spectral properties using the extended Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz. For a Hubbard cluster, we compare ground-state spectral functions obtained with the extended Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz against those from standard Kadanoff--Baym equations. At moderate interaction strengths, the spectral functions show good agreement. At large interaction strengths, however, significant deviations emerge, and the spectral function obtained with the extended Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz becomes negative. These results demonstrate that, despite its computational advantages, the Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz has important limitations in strongly interacting regimes and may produce unphysical spectral properties when interactions become sufficiently large.

</details>


### [111] [Green's Functions from Sample-based Krylov Quantum Diagonalization: An Impurity Solver for Dynamical Mean-Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09147)
*Jay Patel,Chakradhar Rangi,Ka-Ming Tam*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文将基于采样的Krylov量子对角化方法从基态计算推广到单粒子格林函数评估，通过构造和采样N±1粒子数扇区中的酉Krylov子空间，并经典地评估所有连接扇区的重叠，利用Lanczos连分数重建格林函数，同时保持了SKQD的浅电路、无辅助比特特性。在粒子-空穴对称的单杂质安德森模型上的数值实验表明，该方法在跨越金属-绝缘体转变的相互作用强度范围内能够复现主要光谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态平均场理论(DMFT)中的杂质求解器在近期量子硬件上受限于可用的浴位点数。现有基于量子计算的格林函数方法通常需要较深的电路或辅助量子比特，限制了其在近斯量子设备上的实用性和可扩展性。因此需要一种既能保持浅电路特性又能可靠计算单粒子格林函数的方法。

Method: 将样本式Krylov量子对角化(SKQD)方法从基态问题推广到单粒子格林函数计算。具体做法是在N+1和N-1粒子数扇区构造并采样酉Krylov子空间，所有扇区连接的重叠矩阵元通过经典计算评估，最终通过Lanczos连分数形式重建格林函数。量子设备仅需制备和采样短时间的动力学演化，保持SKQD原有的浅电路和无辅助比特优势。

Result: 在链式几何的粒子-空穴对称单杂质安德森模型上，采用DMFT中使用的离散浴表示，该方法仅用全希尔伯特空间的一小部分即可恢复谱函数。在跨越金属-绝缘体转变的相互强度范围内，主要光谱特征均得到了复现。

Conclusion: 该研究结果表明，基于SKQD的格林函数计算方法有望在近期量子硬件上实现比当前方法更大浴位点数的DMFT杂质求解器，为量子计算在强关联电子体系模拟中的实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We generalize the sample-based Krylov quantum diagonalization (SKQD) method from ground-state calculations to the evaluation of single-particle Green's functions. By constructing and sampling unitary Krylov subspaces in the N +/- 1 particle-number sectors and evaluating all sector-connecting overlaps classically, the approach reconstructs the Green's function via a Lanczos continued fraction while retaining the shallow-circuit, ancilla-free character of SKQD. The quantum device is required only to prepare and sample short-time evolutions. Applied to the particle-hole-symmetric single-impurity Anderson model in chain geometry, with the discrete bath representation used in dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), the method recovers the spectral function using a relatively small fraction of the full Hilbert space. Across a range of interaction strengths that spans the metal-insulator transition, the main spectral features are reproduced. These results suggest that SKQD-based Green's-function calculations may allow DMFT impurity solvers with a larger number of bath sites on near-term quantum hardware than is currently practical.

</details>
